244 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, n);

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 335-389
Documentation:

Method code:
335 |   protected int getChar() {
336 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
337 |       cursor++;
338 |       --ungetCursor;
339 |       if (charno == -1) {
340 |         charno = getOffset();
341 |       }
342 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
343 |     }
344 | 
345 |     for (;;) {
346 |       int c;
347 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
348 |         hitEOF = true;
349 |         if (charno == -1) {
350 |           charno = getOffset();
351 |         }
352 |         return EOF_CHAR;
353 |       }
354 |       cursor++;
355 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
356 | 
357 | 
358 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
359 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
360 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
361 |           continue;
362 |         }
363 |         lineEndChar = -1;
364 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
365 |         lineno++;
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       if (c <= 127) {
369 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
370 |           lineEndChar = c;
371 |           c = '\n';
372 |         }
373 |       } else {
374 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
375 |           continue;
376 |         }
377 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
378 |           lineEndChar = c;
379 |           c = '\n';
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       if (charno == -1) {
384 |         charno = getOffset();
385 |       }
386 | 
387 |       return c;
388 |     }
389 |   }

703 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
704 |           charno = stream.getCharno();
705 |           type = null;
706 |

708 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(
709 |                 parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
710 | 
711 |             if (type == null) {
712 |               // parsing error reported during recursive descent
713 |               // recovering parsing
714 |               return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
715 |             }
716 |             skipEOLs();
717 |             token = next();
718 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
719 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
720 |           }
721 | 
722 |           String name = null;
723 |           boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
724 |           if (isBracketedParam) {
725 |             token = next();
726 |           }
727 | 
728 |           if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
729 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
730 |                 lineno, charno);
731 |           } else {
732 |             name = stream.getString();
733 | 
734 |             if (isBracketedParam) {
735 |               token = next();
736 | 
737 |               // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
738 |               // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
739 |               // system.
740 |               if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
741 |                 token = next();
742 |                 if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
743 |                   token = next();
744 |                 }
745 |               }
746 | 
747 |               if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
748 |                 reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
749 |               } else if (type != null) {
750 |                 // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
751 |                 // already.
752 |                 type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
753 |               }
754 |             }
755 | 
756 |             // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
757 |             // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
758 |             // for handling properties of params.
759 |             if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
760 |               name = null;
761 |             } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
762 |               if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
763 |                 parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
764 |                     lineno, charno);
765 |               } else {
766 |                 parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
767 |                     lineno, charno);
768 |               }
769 |             }
770 |           }
771 | 
772 |           if (name == null) {
773 |             token = eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation(token);
774 |             return token;
775 |           }
776 | 
777 |           jsdocBuilder.markName(name, sourceFile, lineno, charno);
778 | 
779 |           // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
780 |           if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()
781 |               && token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION) {
782 |             ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
783 |                 extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
784 | 
785 |             String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
786 | 
787 |             if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
788 |               jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
789 |                   paramDescription);
790 |             }
791 | 
792 |             token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
793 |           } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOC && token != JsDocToken.EOF) {
794 |             token = eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
795 |           }
796 |           return token;
797 | 
798 |         case PRESERVE_TRY:
799 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
800 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
801 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
802 |           }
803 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
804 | 
805 |         case NO_SHADOW:
806 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
807 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
808 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
809 |           }
810 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
811 | 
812 |         case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
813 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
814 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
815 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
816 |           }
817 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
818 | 
819 |         case MODIFIES:
820 |           token = parseModifiesTag(next());
821 |           return token;
822 | 
823 |         case IMPLICIT_CAST:
824 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
825 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
826 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
827 |           }
828 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
829 | 
830 |         case SEE:
831 |           if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
832 |             ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
833 |             String reference = referenceInfo.string;
834 | 
835 |             if (reference.length() == 0) {
836 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
837 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
838 |             } else {

840 |             }

843 |           } else {
844 |             token = eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
845 |           }
846 |           return token;
847 | 
848 |         case STABLEIDGENERATOR:
849 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordStableIdGenerator()) {
850 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.stableidgen",
851 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
852 |           }
853 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();

856 |           token = parseSuppressTag(next());
857 |           return token;

862 |               Splitter.on(',')
863 |                   .trimResults()
864 |                   .split(templateInfo.string));
865 | 
866 |           if (names.size() == 0 || names.get(0).length() == 0) {
867 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
868 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

870 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
871 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
872 |           }

875 |           return token;
876 |         }

879 |           token = parseIdGeneratorTag(next());
880 |           return token;
881 | 
882 |         case WIZACTION:
883 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordWizaction()) {
884 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.wizaction",
885 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
886 |           }
887 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
888 | 
889 |         case DISPOSES: {
890 |           ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
891 |           List<String> names = Lists.newArrayList(
892 |               Splitter.on(',')
893 |                   .trimResults()
894 |                   .split(templateInfo.string));
895 | 
896 |           if (names.size() == 0 || names.get(0).length() == 0) {
897 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.disposeparameter.missing",
898 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
899 |           } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDisposesParameter(names)) {
900 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.disposeparameter.error",
901 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
902 |           }

905 |           return token;
906 |         }
907 |

909 |           ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
910 |           String version = versionInfo.string;
911 | 
912 |           if (version.length() == 0) {
913 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
914 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
915 |           } else {
916 |             if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
917 |                parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
918 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
919 |             }
920 |           }
921 | 
922 |           token = versionInfo.token;
923 |           return token;
924 | 
925 |         case CONSTANT:
926 |         case DEFINE:
927 |         case RETURN:
928 |         case PRIVATE:
929 |         case PROTECTED:
930 |         case PUBLIC:
931 |         case THIS:
932 |         case TYPE:
933 |         case TYPEDEF:
934 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
935 |           charno = stream.getCharno();
936 | 
937 |           Node typeNode = null;
938 |           boolean hasType = lookAheadForType();
939 |           boolean isAlternateTypeAnnotation =
940 |               (annotation == Annotation.PRIVATE ||
941 |                annotation == Annotation.PROTECTED ||
942 |                annotation == Annotation.PUBLIC ||
943 |                annotation == Annotation.CONSTANT);
944 |           boolean canSkipTypeAnnotation =
945 |               (isAlternateTypeAnnotation ||
946 |                annotation == Annotation.RETURN);
947 |           type = null;
948 |           if (hasType || !canSkipTypeAnnotation) {
949 |             skipEOLs();
950 |             token = next();
951 |             typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
952 | 
953 |             if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
954 |               typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
955 |             }
956 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
957 |           }
958 | 
959 |           // The error was reported during recursive descent
960 |           // recovering parsing
961 |           boolean hasError = type == null && !canSkipTypeAnnotation;
962 |           if (!hasError) {
963 |             // Record types for @type.
964 |             // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
965 |             // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
966 |             // @type {type}\n@private
967 |             // This will have some weird behavior in some cases
968 |             // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
969 |             // but should be mostly OK.
970 |             if ((type != null && isAlternateTypeAnnotation)
971 |                 || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
972 |               if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
973 |                 parser.addTypeWarning(
974 |                     "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
975 |               }
976 |             }
977 | 
978 |             switch (annotation) {
979 |               case CONSTANT:
980 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
981 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
982 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
983 |                 }
984 |                 break;
985 | 
986 |               case DEFINE:
987 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
988 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
989 |                       lineno, charno);
990 |                 }
991 |                 return recordDescription(token);
992 | 
993 |               case PRIVATE:
994 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
995 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
996 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
997 |                       lineno, charno);
998 |                 }
999 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1000 | 
1001 |               case PROTECTED:
1002 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
1003 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
1004 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
1005 |                       lineno, charno);
1006 |                 }
1007 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1008 | 
1009 |               case PUBLIC:
1010 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
1011 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
1012 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
1013 |                       lineno, charno);
1014 |                 }
1015 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1016 | 
1017 |               case RETURN:
1018 |                 if (type == null) {
1019 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(newNode(Token.QMARK));
1020 |                 }
1021 | 
1022 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
1023 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1024 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1025 |                   break;
1026 |                 }
1027 | 
1028 |                 // TODO(johnlenz): The extractMultilineTextualBlock method
1029 |                 // and friends look directly at the stream, regardless of
1030 |                 // last token read, so we don't want to read the first
1031 |                 // "STRING" out of the stream.
1032 | 
1033 |                 boolean isAnnotationNext = lookAheadForAnnotation();
1034 | 
1035 |                 // Find the return's description (if applicable).
1036 |                 if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()
1037 |                     && !isAnnotationNext) {
1038 |                   ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
1039 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
1040 | 
1041 |                   String returnDescription =
1042 |                       returnDescriptionInfo.string;
1043 | 
1044 |                   if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
1045 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
1046 |                         returnDescription);
1047 |                   }
1048 | 
1049 |                   token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
1050 |                 } else {
1051 |                   token = eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
1052 |                 }
1053 |                 return token;
1054 | 
1055 |               case THIS:
1056 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
1057 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1058 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1059 |                 }
1060 |                 break;
1061 | 
1062 |               case TYPEDEF:
1063 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
1064 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1065 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1066 |                 }
1067 |                 break;
1068 |             }
1069 |           }
1070 | 
1071 |           return eatUntilEOLIfNotAnnotation();
1072 |       }
1073 |     }
1074 | 
1075 |     return next();
1076 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 529-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 630-642
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
630 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
631 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
632 |     if (typeSet == null) {
633 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
634 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
638 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
639 | 
640 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
641 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
642 |   }

===== M0023 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 72-107
Documentation:

Method code:
72 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
73 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
74 |       if (inputId == null) {
75 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
76 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
77 |         // from the AST.
78 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
79 |       }
80 | 
81 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
82 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
83 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
84 | 
85 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
86 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
87 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
88 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
89 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       // Args: Declare function variables
93 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
94 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
95 |            a = a.getNext()) {
96 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
97 |         declareVar(a);
98 |       }
99 | 
100 |       // Body
101 |       scanVars(body);
102 |     } else {
103 |       // It's the global block
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
105 |       scanVars(n);
106 |     }
107 |   }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2101-2116
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2101 |   public static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2102 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2103 |         n.isGetElem());
2104 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2105 |     if (parent == null) {
2106 |       return false;
2107 |     }
2108 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2109 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2110 |         || parent.isVar()
2111 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2112 |         || parent.isDec()
2113 |         || parent.isInc()
2114 |         || parent.isParamList()
2115 |         || parent.isCatch();
2116 |   }

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1518-1569

/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if

   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.

1520 |     if (type != node.getType()
1521 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1522 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1523 |       return false;
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1527 |       return false;
1528 |     }
1529 | 
1530 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1531 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1532 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1533 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1534 |         return false;
1535 |       }
1536 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1537 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1538 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1539 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1540 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1541 |           return false;
1542 |         }
1543 |       }
1544 | 
1545 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1546 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1547 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1548 |         return false;
1549 |       }
1550 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1551 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1552 |         return false;
1553 |       }
1554 |     }
1555 | 
1556 |     if (recur) {
1557 |       Node n, n2;
1558 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1559 |            n != null;
1560 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1561 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1562 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1563 |           return false;
1564 |         }
1565 |       }
1566 |     }
1567 | 
1568 |     return true;
1569 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;

98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

===== M0066 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
170 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
175 |       if (first.isName() &&
176 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
177 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
178 |       }
179 |     }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 785-868
Documentation:

Method code:
785 |     @Override
786 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
787 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
788 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
789 |       if (name == null) {
790 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
791 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
792 |           errorReporter.error(
793 |             "unnamed function statement",
794 |             sourceName,
795 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
796 | 
797 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
798 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
799 |         }
800 |         name = new Name();
801 |         name.setIdentifier("");
802 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
803 |       }
804 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
805 |       // if the function has an inline return annotation, attach it
806 |       Node newName = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(name);
807 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
808 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
809 |         // declaration.
810 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
811 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
812 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
813 |         // same line as the function keyword.
814 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
815 |             functionNode.getLp();
816 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
817 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
818 |       }
819 | 
820 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
821 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
822 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
823 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
824 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
825 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
826 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
827 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
828 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
829 |       if (fnName != null) {
830 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
831 |       } else {
832 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
833 |       }
834 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
835 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
836 | 
837 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
838 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
839 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
840 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
841 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
842 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
843 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
844 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
845 |         } else {
846 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
847 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
848 |           // (an error has already been reported).
849 |           Preconditions.checkState(
850 |               config.isIdeMode
851 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
852 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
853 |         }
854 |       }
855 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
856 | 
857 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
858 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
859 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
860 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
861 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
862 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
863 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
864 |       }
865 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
866 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
867 |      return node;
868 |     }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1248-1250
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1248 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1249 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1250 |   }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.JSDocInfo.getTypeNodes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/JSDocInfo.java
Original range: 1595-1641
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a collection of all type nodes that are a part of this JSDocInfo.
   * This includes @type, @this, @extends, @implements, @param, @throws,
   * and @return.  Any future type specific JSDoc should make sure to add the
   * appropriate nodes here.
   * @return collection of all type nodes
   */
Method code:
1595 |   public Collection<Node> getTypeNodes() {
1596 |     List<Node> nodes = Lists.newArrayList();
1597 | 
1598 |     if (type != null) {
1599 |       nodes.add(type.getRoot());
1600 |     }
1601 | 
1602 |     if (thisType != null) {
1603 |       nodes.add(thisType.getRoot());
1604 |     }
1605 | 
1606 |     if (info != null) {
1607 |       if (info.baseType != null) {
1608 |         nodes.add(info.baseType.getRoot());
1609 |       }
1610 | 
1611 |       if (info.extendedInterfaces != null) {
1612 |         for (JSTypeExpression interfaceType : info.extendedInterfaces) {
1613 |           nodes.add(interfaceType.getRoot());
1614 |         }
1615 |       }
1616 | 
1617 |       if (info.implementedInterfaces != null) {
1618 |         for (JSTypeExpression interfaceType : info.implementedInterfaces) {
1619 |           nodes.add(interfaceType.getRoot());
1620 |         }
1621 |       }
1622 | 
1623 |       if (info.parameters != null) {
1624 |         for (JSTypeExpression parameterType : info.parameters.values()) {
1625 |           if (parameterType != null) {
1626 |             nodes.add(parameterType.getRoot());
1627 |           }
1628 |         }
1629 |       }
1630 | 
1631 |       if (info.thrownTypes != null) {
1632 |         for (JSTypeExpression thrownType : info.thrownTypes) {
1633 |           if (thrownType != null) {
1634 |             nodes.add(thrownType.getRoot());
1635 |           }
1636 |         }
1637 |       }
1638 |     }
1639 | 
1640 |     return nodes;
1641 |   }

157 |   public void markAnnotation(String annotation, int lineno, int charno) {

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-646
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
571 |       final JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
572 |     if (this == that) {
573 |       return true;
574 |     }
575 | 
576 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
577 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
578 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
579 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
580 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
581 |         // with everyone.
582 |         return true;
583 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
584 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
585 |         // both unknown.
586 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
587 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
588 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
589 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
590 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
591 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
592 |         return false;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
597 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
598 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
602 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
603 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
607 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
608 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
612 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
613 |       return false;
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
617 |       // TODO(johnlenz): is this valid across scopes?
618 |       return getConcreteNominalTypeName(this.toObjectType()).equals(
619 |           getConcreteNominalTypeName(that.toObjectType()));
620 |     }
621 | 
622 |     if (isTemplateType() && that.isTemplateType()) {
623 |       // TemplateType are they same only if they are object identical,
624 |       // which we check at the start of this function.
625 |       return false;
626 |     }
627 | 
628 |     // Unbox other proxies.
629 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
630 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
631 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
632 |               that, eqMethod);
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
636 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
637 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
638 |           eqMethod);
639 |     }
640 | 
641 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
642 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
643 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
644 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
645 |     return false;
646 |   }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 318-357
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
318 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
319 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
320 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
321 |       options.checkTypes = true;
322 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
323 |       options.checkTypes = false;
324 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
325 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
326 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
327 |       // parser type warnings.
328 |       options.setWarningLevel(
329 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
330 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
331 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
335 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
336 |       options.setWarningLevel(
337 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
338 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
339 |     }
340 | 
341 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
342 |       options.setWarningLevel(
343 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
344 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
348 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
349 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
350 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
351 |     // error off.
352 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
353 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
354 |       options.setWarningLevel(
355 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
356 |     }
357 |   }

   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

199 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
200 |                                    String sourceString,
201 |                                    Config config,
202 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
203 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
204 |         config, errorReporter);
205 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
206 | 
207 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
208 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
209 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
210 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {

212 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
213 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);

216 |     }
217 |

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.findAliases(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 357-445
Documentation:

Method code:
357 |     private void findAliases(NodeTraversal t) {
358 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
359 |       for (Var v : scope.getVarIterable()) {
360 |         Node n = v.getNode();
361 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
362 |         boolean isVar = parent.isVar();
363 |         boolean isFunctionDecl = NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent);
364 |         if (isVar && n.getFirstChild() != null && n.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
365 |           recordAlias(v);
366 |         } else if (v.isBleedingFunction()) {
367 |           // Bleeding functions already get a BAD_PARAMETERS error, so just
368 |           // do nothing.
369 |         } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
370 |           // Parameters of the scope function also get a BAD_PARAMETERS
371 |           // error.
372 |         } else if (isVar || isFunctionDecl) {
373 |           boolean isHoisted = NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(parent);
374 |           Node grandparent = parent.getParent();
375 |           Node value = v.getInitialValue() != null ?
376 |               v.getInitialValue() :
377 |               null;
378 |           Node varNode = null;
379 | 
380 |           String name = n.getString();
381 |           int nameCount = scopedAliasNames.count(name);
382 |           scopedAliasNames.add(name);
383 |           String globalName =
384 |               "$jscomp.scope." + name + (nameCount == 0 ? "" : ("$" + nameCount));
385 | 
386 |           compiler.ensureLibraryInjected("base");
387 | 
388 |           // First, we need to free up the function expression (EXPR)
389 |           // to be used in another expression.
390 |           if (isFunctionDecl) {
391 |             // Replace "function NAME() { ... }" with "var NAME;".
392 |             Node existingName = v.getNameNode();
393 | 
394 |             // We can't keep the local name on the function expression,
395 |             // because IE is buggy and will leak the name into the global
396 |             // scope. This is covered in more detail here:
397 |             // http://wiki.ecmascript.org/lib/exe/fetch.php?id=resources:resources&cache=cache&media=resources:jscriptdeviationsfromes3.pdf
398 |             //
399 |             // This will only cause problems if this is a hoisted, recursive
400 |             // function, and the programmer is using the hoisting.
401 |             Node newName = IR.name("").useSourceInfoFrom(existingName);
402 |             value.replaceChild(existingName, newName);
403 | 
404 |             varNode = IR.var(existingName).useSourceInfoFrom(existingName);
405 |             grandparent.replaceChild(parent, varNode);
406 |           } else {
407 |             if (value != null) {
408 |               // If this is a VAR, we can just detach the expression and
409 |               // the tree will still be valid.
410 |               value.detachFromParent();
411 |             }
412 |             varNode = parent;
413 |           }
414 | 
415 |           // Add $jscomp.scope.name = EXPR;
416 |           // Make sure we copy over all the jsdoc and debug info.
417 |           if (value != null || v.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
418 |             Node newDecl = NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNodeDeclaration(
419 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(),
420 |                 globalName,
421 |                 value,
422 |                 v.getJSDocInfo())
423 |                 .useSourceInfoIfMissingFromForTree(n);
424 |             NodeUtil.setDebugInformation(
425 |                 newDecl.getFirstChild().getFirstChild(), n, name);
426 | 
427 |             if (isHoisted) {
428 |               grandparent.addChildToFront(newDecl);
429 |             } else {
430 |               grandparent.addChildBefore(newDecl, varNode);
431 |             }
432 |           }
433 | 
434 |           // Rewrite "var name = EXPR;" to "var name = $jscomp.scope.name;"
435 |           v.getNameNode().addChildToFront(
436 |               NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNode(
437 |                   compiler.getCodingConvention(), globalName, n, name));
438 | 
439 |           recordAlias(v);
440 |         } else {
441 |           // Do not other kinds of local symbols, like catch params.
442 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_NON_ALIAS_LOCAL, n.getString());
443 |         }
444 |       }
445 |     }

Method code:
384 |     @Override
385 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
386 |       switch (n.getType()) {
387 |         case Token.WHILE:
388 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
389 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
390 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
391 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
392 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
393 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
394 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
395 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
396 |           }
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
400 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
401 |           break;
402 | 
403 |         case Token.NAME:
404 |         case Token.STRING:
405 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
406 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
407 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
408 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
409 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
410 |           }
411 |           break;
412 | 
413 |         case Token.CAST:
414 |           parent.replaceChild(n, n.removeFirstChild());
415 |           break;
416 |       }

671 |   public void addChildrenAfter(Node children, Node node) {
672 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node == null || node.parent == this);

===== M0206 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1256-1316
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1256 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1257 |     // unknown
1258 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1259 |       return true;
1260 |     }
1261 |     // all type
1262 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1263 |       return true;
1264 |     }
1265 |     // equality
1266 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1267 |       return true;
1268 |     }
1269 |     // unions
1270 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1271 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1272 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1273 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1274 |           return true;
1275 |         }
1276 |       }
1277 |       return false;
1278 |     }
1279 | 
1280 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1281 |     // are not equivalent.
1282 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1283 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1284 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1285 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1286 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1287 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1288 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1289 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1290 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1291 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1292 | 
1293 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1294 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1295 |     } else {
1296 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1297 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1298 |     }
1299 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1300 |       return false;
1301 |     }
1302 | 
1303 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1304 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1305 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1306 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1307 |               thatType);
1308 |     }
1309 | 
1310 |     // proxy types
1311 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1312 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1313 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1314 |     }
1315 |     return false;
1316 |   }

===== M0221 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 624-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
624 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
625 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
626 |         next = c.getNext();
627 |         if (c.isVar()) {
628 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
629 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
630 |           }
631 | 
632 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
633 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
634 |             c.removeChild(name);
635 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
636 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
637 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
638 |           }
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
119 | 
120 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
121 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
122 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
123 | 
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
147 |   }

===== M0267 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 567-580
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
568 |       Node ret;
569 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
570 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
571 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
572 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
573 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
574 |       } else {
575 |         ret = transform(n);
576 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
577 |       }
578 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
579 |       return ret;
580 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.annotateConstantsByConvention(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 422-452
Documentation:
/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
422 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
423 |       Preconditions.checkState(
424 |           n.isName()
425 |           || n.isString()
426 |           || n.isStringKey()
427 |           || n.isGetterDef()
428 |           || n.isSetterDef());
429 | 
430 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
431 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
432 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
433 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n);
434 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
435 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
436 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
437 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
438 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
439 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
440 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
441 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
442 |           if (assertOnChange) {
443 |             String name = n.getString();
444 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
445 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
446 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
447 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
448 |           }
449 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 81-110
Documentation:

Method code:
81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     int startErrorCount = compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount();
83 |     try {
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null ||
97 |         // Most passes try to report as many errors as possible,
98 |         // so there may already be errors. We only care if there were
99 |         // errors in the code we just parsed.
100 |         (compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount() > startErrorCount && !compiler.isIdeMode())) {
101 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
102 |       root = IR.script();
103 |     } else {
104 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
108 |     // the source file and module.
109 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
110 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1919-1945
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1919 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1920 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1921 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1922 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1923 |       skipEOLs();
1924 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1925 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1926 |       skipEOLs();
1927 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1928 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1929 |       skipEOLs();
1930 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1931 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1932 |       String string = stream.getString();
1933 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1934 |         skipEOLs();
1935 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1936 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1937 |         return newStringNode(string);
1938 |       } else {
1939 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1940 |       }
1941 |     }
1942 | 
1943 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1944 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1945 |   }

   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *

   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.

   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   */

1951 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1952 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1953 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1954 |     }
1955 | 
1956 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1957 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1958 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1959 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1960 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1961 |       skipEOLs();
1962 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1963 |         next();
1964 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1965 |       }
1966 |     }
1967 | 
1968 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1969 | 
1970 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1971 |       next();
1972 |       skipEOLs();
1973 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1974 |       if (memberType != null) {
1975 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1976 | 
1977 |         skipEOLs();
1978 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1979 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1980 |         }
1981 | 
1982 |         next();
1983 |       }
1984 |     }
1985 |     return typeNameNode;
1986 |   }

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1324-1448
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1324 |   Node parseInputs() {
1325 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1326 | 
1327 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1328 |     // individual file parse trees.
1329 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1330 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1331 |     }
1332 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1333 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1334 |     }
1335 | 
1336 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1337 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1338 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1339 | 
1340 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1341 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1342 | 
1343 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1344 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1345 | 
1346 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1347 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1348 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1349 |     }
1350 | 
1351 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1352 | 
1353 |     try {
1354 |       // Parse externs sources.
1355 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1356 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1357 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1358 |           return null;
1359 |         }
1360 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1361 |       }
1362 | 
1363 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1364 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1365 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1366 |       }
1367 | 
1368 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1369 | 
1370 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1371 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1372 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1373 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1374 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1375 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1376 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1377 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1378 |           }
1379 |         }
1380 | 
1381 |         try {
1382 |           inputs =
1383 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1384 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1385 |           staleInputs = true;
1386 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1387 |           report(JSError.make(
1388 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1389 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1390 |           report(JSError.make(
1391 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1392 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1393 |           report(JSError.make(
1394 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1395 |         }
1396 | 
1397 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1398 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1399 |           return null;
1400 |         }
1401 |       }
1402 | 
1403 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1404 | 
1405 |       if (staleInputs) {
1406 |         repartitionInputs();
1407 |       }
1408 | 
1409 |       // Build the AST.
1410 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1411 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1412 |         if (n == null) {
1413 |           continue;
1414 |         }
1415 | 
1416 |         if (devMode) {
1417 |           runSanityCheck();
1418 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1419 |             return null;
1420 |           }
1421 |         }
1422 | 
1423 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1424 |         // externExportsPath.
1425 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1426 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1427 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1428 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1429 | 
1430 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1431 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1432 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1433 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1434 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1435 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1436 |         }
1437 | 
1438 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1439 |       }
1440 | 
1441 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1442 |         return null;
1443 |       }
1444 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1445 |     } finally {
1446 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1447 |     }
1448 |   }

391 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
392 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
393 |       cursor++;
394 |       --ungetCursor;
395 |       if (charno == -1) {
396 |         charno = getOffset();
397 |       }
398 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
399 |     }
400 | 
401 |     for (;;) {
402 |       int c;
403 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
404 |         hitEOF = true;
405 |         if (charno == -1) {
406 |           charno = getOffset();
407 |         }
408 |         return EOF_CHAR;
409 |       }
410 |       cursor++;
411 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
412 | 
413 | 
414 |       if (c <= 127) {
415 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
416 |           lineEndChar = c;
417 |           c = '\n';
418 |         }
419 |       } else {
420 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
421 |           continue;
422 |         }
423 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
424 |           lineEndChar = c;
425 |           c = '\n';
426 |         }
427 |       }

430 |         charno = getOffset();
431 |       }
432 |

434 |     }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenamer.addDeclaredName(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 461-478
Documentation:
/**
     * Adds a name to the map of names declared in this scope.
     */
Method code:
461 |     @Override
462 |     public void addDeclaredName(String name) {
463 |       if (!name.equals(ARGUMENTS)) {
464 |         if (global) {
465 |           reserveName(name);
466 |         } else {
467 |           // It hasn't been declared locally yet, so increment the count.
468 |           if (!declarations.containsKey(name)) {
469 |             int id = incrementNameCount(name);
470 |             String newName = null;
471 |             if (id != 0) {

476 |         }
477 |       }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1807-1823
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1807 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1808 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1809 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1810 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1811 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1812 |         next();
1813 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1814 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1815 |           next();
1816 |         }
1817 |         skipEOLs();
1818 |         token = next();
1819 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1820 |       }
1821 |     }
1822 |     return typeExpr;
1823 |   }

===== M0423 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }
