Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 120-285
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

Documentation:
/** Whether the given name is constant by coding convention. */
Method code:
2895 |   static boolean isConstantByConvention(
2896 |       CodingConvention convention, Node node, Node parent) {
2897 |     if (parent.isGetProp() && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
2898 |       return convention.isConstantKey(node.getString());

2903 |     }
2904 |     return false;

149 |   private IRFactory(String sourceString,

152 |                     ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
153 |     this.sourceString = sourceString;
154 |     this.newlines = Lists.newArrayList();
155 |     this.sourceFile = sourceFile;
156 | 
157 |     // Pre-generate all the newlines in the file.
158 |     for (int charNo = 0; true; charNo++) {
159 |       charNo = sourceString.indexOf('\n', charNo);
160 |       if (charNo == -1) break;
161 |       newlines.add(Integer.valueOf(charNo));
162 |     }
163 | 
164 |     // Sometimes this will be null in tests.
165 |     this.sourceName = sourceFile == null ? null : sourceFile.getName();
166 |

168 |     this.errorReporter = errorReporter;
169 |     this.transformDispatcher = new TransformDispatcher();
170 |     // The template node properties are applied to all nodes in this transform.
171 |     this.templateNode = createTemplateNode();
172 | 
173 |     switch (config.languageMode) {
174 |       case ECMASCRIPT3:
175 |         reservedKeywords = null; // use TokenStream.isKeyword instead
176 |         break;
177 |       case ECMASCRIPT5:
178 |         reservedKeywords = ES5_RESERVED_KEYWORDS;
179 |         break;
180 |       case ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT:
181 |         reservedKeywords = ES5_STRICT_RESERVED_KEYWORDS;
182 |         break;
183 |       default:
184 |         throw new IllegalStateException("unknown language mode");
185 |     }

Method code:
775 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
776 |     boolean valid = false;
777 |     if (i == 0) {
778 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
779 |     } else if (i == 1) {
780 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
781 |     } else {
782 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
783 |     }
784 |     if (!valid) {
785 |       violation(
786 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
787 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
788 |     }
789 |   }

Method code:
278 |   private static boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
279 |     switch (c) {
280 |       case '@':
281 |       case '*':
282 |       case ',':
283 |       case '>':
284 |       case ':':
285 |       case '(':
286 |       case ')':
287 |       case '{':
288 |       case '}':
289 |       case '[':
290 |       case ']':
291 |       case '?':
292 |       case '!':
293 |       case '|':
294 |       case '=':
295 |       case EOF_CHAR:
296 |       case '\n':
297 |         return false;
298 | 
299 |       default:
300 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
301 |     }
302 |   }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 216-239
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
216 |   boolean parse() {
217 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
218 |     skipEOLs();
219 | 
220 |     JsDocToken token = next();
221 | 
222 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
223 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
224 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
225 |       token = blockInfo.token;
226 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
227 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
228 |       }
229 |     } else {
230 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
231 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
232 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
233 |         // what it was.
234 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
235 |       }
236 |     }
237 | 
238 |     return parseHelperLoop(token, Lists.<ExtendedTypeInfo>newArrayList());
239 |   }

633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
646 |   }

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

===== M0484 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

Method code:
308 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
309 |     if (c <= 127) {
310 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
311 |     } else {
312 |       return c == 0xA0
313 |           || Character.getType((char) c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
314 |     }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNode(CodingConvention,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2350-2378
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a node representing a qualified name.
   *
   * @param name A qualified name (e.g. "foo" or "foo.bar.baz")
   * @return A NAME or GETPROP node
   */
Method code:
2350 |   public static Node newQualifiedNameNode(
2351 |       CodingConvention convention, String name) {
2352 |     int endPos = name.indexOf('.');
2353 |     if (endPos == -1) {
2354 |       return newName(convention, name);
2355 |     }
2356 |     Node node;
2357 |     String nodeName = name.substring(0, endPos);
2358 |     if ("this".equals(nodeName)) {
2359 |       node = IR.thisNode();
2360 |     } else {
2361 |       node = newName(convention, nodeName);
2362 |     }
2363 |     int startPos;
2364 |     do {
2365 |       startPos = endPos + 1;
2366 |       endPos = name.indexOf('.', startPos);
2367 |       String part = (endPos == -1
2368 |                      ? name.substring(startPos)
2369 |                      : name.substring(startPos, endPos));
2370 |       Node propNode = IR.string(part);
2371 |       if (convention.isConstantKey(part)) {
2372 |         propNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
2373 |       }
2374 |       node = IR.getprop(node, propNode);
2375 |     } while (endPos != -1);
2376 | 
2377 |     return node;
2378 |   }

/**
   * Creates an object type, allowing specification of the implicit prototype,
   * whether the object is native, and any templatized types.

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 628-643
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
628 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
629 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
630 |       Set<String> directives = null;
631 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
632 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
633 |         if (directives == null) {
634 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
635 |         } else {
636 |           directives.add(directive);
637 |         }
638 |       }
639 | 
640 |       if (directives != null) {
641 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
642 |       }
643 |     }

===== M0523 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 567-614
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private void extractForInitializer(
568 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
569 | 
570 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
571 |         next = c.getNext();
572 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
573 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
574 |         switch (c.getType()) {
575 |           case Token.LABEL:
576 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
577 |             break;
578 |           case Token.FOR:
579 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
580 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
581 |               if (first.isVar()) {
582 |                 // Transform:
583 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
584 |                 // to:
585 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
586 |                 Node newStatement = first;
587 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
588 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
589 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
590 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
591 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
592 |               }
593 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
594 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
595 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
596 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
597 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
598 | 
599 |               Node newStatement;
600 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
601 |               // but are handled differently.
602 |               if (init.isVar()) {
603 |                 newStatement = init;
604 |               } else {
605 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
606 |               }
607 | 
608 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
609 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
610 |             }
611 |             break;
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 |     }

===== M0533 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 112-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
112 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
113 |     switch (n.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.VAR:
115 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
116 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |              child != null;) {
118 |           Node next = child.getNext();
119 |           declareVar(child);
120 |           child = next;
121 |         }
122 |         return;
123 | 
124 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
125 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
126 |           return;
127 |         }
128 | 
129 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
130 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
131 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
135 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
136 | 
137 |       case Token.CATCH:
138 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
140 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
141 |         // is the code block
142 | 
143 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
144 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
145 | 
146 |         declareVar(var);
147 |         scanVars(block);
148 |         return;  // only one child to scan
149 | 
150 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
151 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
152 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0536 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadFor(char)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2566-2582
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * @return Whether we found the char.
   */
Method code:
2566 |   private boolean lookAheadFor(char expect) {
2567 |     boolean matched = false;
2568 |     int c;
2569 |     while (true) {
2570 |       c = stream.getChar();
2571 |       if (c == ' ') {
2572 |         continue;
2573 |       } else if (c == expect) {
2574 |         matched = true;
2575 |         break;
2576 |       } else {
2577 |         break;
2578 |       }
2579 |     }
2580 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2581 |     return matched;
2582 |   }

===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.useSourceInfoIfMissingFromForTree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1828-1836
Documentation:
/**
   * Overwrite all the source information in this node and its subtree with
   * that of {@code other} iff the source info is missing.
   */
Method code:
1828 |   public Node useSourceInfoIfMissingFromForTree(Node other) {
1829 |     useSourceInfoIfMissingFrom(other);
1830 |     for (Node child = getFirstChild();
1831 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()) {

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

===== M0551 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.position2charno(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 527-542
Documentation:

Method code:
527 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
528 |     int newlineIndex = Collections.binarySearch(newlines, position);
529 |     int lineIndex = -1;
530 |     if (newlineIndex >= 0) {
531 |       lineIndex = newlines.get(newlineIndex);
532 |     } else if (newlineIndex <= -2) {
533 |       lineIndex = newlines.get(-newlineIndex - 2);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
537 |       return position;
538 |     } else {
539 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
540 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
541 |     }
542 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is explicitly declared by the programmer.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the declaration of property
   *        which might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
284 |   public final boolean defineDeclaredProperty(String propertyName,
285 |       JSType type, Node propertyNode) {
286 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, false, propertyNode);
287 |     // All property definitions go through this method
288 |     // or defineInferredProperty. Because the properties defined an an
289 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient

293 |   }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 644-663
Documentation:

Method code:
644 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
645 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
646 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
647 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
648 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
649 |       if (typeSet == null) {
650 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
651 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
652 |       }
653 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
654 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
655 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
656 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
657 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
658 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
659 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
660 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
661 |       }
662 |     }
663 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1278 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1279 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1280 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1281 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1282 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1283 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1284 |       } else {
1285 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1286 |       }
1287 |     }
1288 |     return builder.build();
1289 |   }

===== M0573 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1856-1901
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1856 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1857 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1858 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1859 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1860 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 8 cases:
1861 |       // {?} - right curly
1862 |       // {?=} - equals
1863 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1864 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1865 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1866 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1867 |       // {Array.<?>} - greater than
1868 |       // /** ? */ - EOC (inline types)
1869 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1870 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1871 |       token = next();
1872 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1873 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1874 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1875 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1876 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1877 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE ||
1878 |           token == JsDocToken.GT ||
1879 |           token == JsDocToken.EOC) {
1880 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1881 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1882 |       }
1883 | 
1884 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1885 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1886 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1887 |     } else {
1888 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1889 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1890 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1891 |           next();
1892 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1893 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1894 |           next();
1895 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1896 |         }
1897 |       }
1898 | 
1899 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1900 |     }
1901 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3113-3141
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3113 |   public static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3114 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3115 |     if (info == null) {
3116 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3117 |       if (parent == null) {
3118 |         return null;
3119 |       }
3120 | 
3121 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3122 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3123 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3124 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3125 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3126 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3127 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3128 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3129 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3130 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3131 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3132 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3133 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3134 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3135 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3136 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3137 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3138 |       }
3139 |     }
3140 |     return info;
3141 |   }

===== M0615 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1579-1594
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1579 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1580 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1581 |       String name = getString();
1582 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1583 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1584 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1585 |       if (left == null) {
1586 |         return null;
1587 |       }
1588 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1589 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1590 |       return "this";
1591 |     } else {
1592 |       return null;
1593 |     }
1594 |   }

===== M0620 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 307-366
Documentation:

Method code:
307 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo info, AstNode node) {
308 |     if (info.hasType()) {
309 |       boolean valid = false;
310 |       switch (node.getType()) {
311 |         // Casts are valid
312 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
313 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
314 |           break;
315 |         // Variable declarations are valid
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
317 |           valid = true;
318 |           break;
319 |         // Function declarations are valid
320 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
321 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode) node;
322 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
323 |           break;
324 |         // Object literal properties, catch declarations and variable
325 |         // initializers are valid.
326 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
327 |           AstNode parent = node.getParent();
328 |           valid = parent instanceof ObjectProperty
329 |               || parent instanceof CatchClause
330 |               || parent instanceof FunctionNode
331 |               || (parent instanceof VariableInitializer &&
332 |                   node == ((VariableInitializer) parent).getTarget());
333 |           break;
334 |         // Object literal properties are valid
335 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
336 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
337 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
338 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
339 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
340 |           break;
341 | 
342 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
343 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
344 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
345 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
346 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment) node).getLeft());
347 |           }
348 |           break;
349 | 
350 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
351 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
352 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
353 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
354 |           break;
355 | 
356 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.CALL:
357 |           valid = info.isDefine();
358 |           break;
359 |       }
360 |       if (!valid) {
361 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
362 |             sourceName,
363 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
364 |       }
365 |     }
366 |   }

===== M0621 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

===== M0665 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1453-1489
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1453 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1454 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1455 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1456 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1457 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1458 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1459 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1460 |           continue;
1461 |         }
1462 |       }
1463 | 
1464 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1465 | 
1466 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1467 |       if (n == null) {
1468 |         continue;
1469 |       }
1470 | 
1471 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1472 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1473 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1474 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1475 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1476 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1477 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1478 | 
1479 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1480 | 
1481 |         externs.add(input);
1482 |         staleInputs = true;
1483 |       }
1484 |     }
1485 | 
1486 |     if (staleInputs) {
1487 |       repartitionInputs();
1488 |     }
1489 |   }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.hotSwapScript(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 131-189
Documentation:

Method code:
131 |   @Override
132 |   public void hotSwapScript(Node root, Node originalRoot) {
133 |     Traversal traversal = new Traversal();
134 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, traversal);
135 | 
136 |     if (!traversal.hasErrors()) {
137 | 
138 |       // Apply the aliases.
139 |       List<AliasUsage> aliasWorkQueue =
140 |           Lists.newArrayList(traversal.getAliasUsages());
141 |       while (!aliasWorkQueue.isEmpty()) {
142 |         List<AliasUsage> newQueue = Lists.newArrayList();
143 |         for (AliasUsage aliasUsage : aliasWorkQueue) {
144 |           if (aliasUsage.referencesOtherAlias()) {
145 |             newQueue.add(aliasUsage);
146 |           } else {
147 |             aliasUsage.applyAlias();
148 |           }
149 |         }
150 | 
151 |         // Prevent an infinite loop.
152 |         if (newQueue.size() == aliasWorkQueue.size()) {
153 |           Var cycleVar = newQueue.get(0).aliasVar;
154 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(
155 |               cycleVar.getNode(), GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_CYCLE, cycleVar.getName()));
156 |           break;
157 |         } else {
158 |           aliasWorkQueue = newQueue;
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 | 
162 |       // Remove the alias definitions.
163 |       for (Node aliasDefinition : traversal.getAliasDefinitionsInOrder()) {
164 |         if (aliasDefinition.getParent().isVar() &&
165 |             aliasDefinition.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
166 |           aliasDefinition.getParent().detachFromParent();
167 |         } else {
168 |           aliasDefinition.detachFromParent();
169 |         }
170 |       }
171 | 
172 |       // Collapse the scopes.
173 |       for (Node scopeCall : traversal.getScopeCalls()) {
174 |         Node expressionWithScopeCall = scopeCall.getParent();
175 |         Node scopeClosureBlock = scopeCall.getLastChild().getLastChild();
176 |         scopeClosureBlock.detachFromParent();
177 |         expressionWithScopeCall.getParent().replaceChild(
178 |             expressionWithScopeCall,
179 |             scopeClosureBlock);
180 |         NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(scopeClosureBlock);
181 |       }
182 | 
183 |       if (traversal.getAliasUsages().size() > 0 ||
184 |           traversal.getAliasDefinitionsInOrder().size() > 0 ||
185 |           traversal.getScopeCalls().size() > 0) {
186 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
187 |       }
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0672 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 426-445
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the function's name. This method recognizes five forms:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code function name() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * </ul>
   * In two last cases with named function expressions, the second name is
   * returned (the variable of qualified name).
   *
   * @param n a node whose type is {@link Token#FUNCTION}
   * @return the function's name, or {@code null} if it has no name
   */
Method code:
426 |   static String getFunctionName(Node n) {
427 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
428 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
429 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
430 |       case Token.NAME:
431 |         // var name = function() ...
432 |         // var name2 = function name1() ...
433 |         return parent.getQualifiedName();
434 | 
435 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
436 |         // qualified.name = function() ...
437 |         // qualified.name2 = function name1() ...
438 |         return parent.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
439 | 
440 |       default:
441 |         // function name() ...
442 |         String name = n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
443 |         return name;
444 |     }
445 |   }

===== M0675 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2000-2002
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
2000 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
2001 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
2002 |   }

===== M0689 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 522-589
Documentation:

Method code:
522 |     @Override
523 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
524 |       if (isCallToScopeMethod(n)) {
525 |         validateScopeCall(t, n, n.getParent());
526 |       }
527 | 
528 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() < 2) {
529 |         return;
530 |       }
531 | 
532 |       int type = n.getType();
533 |       Var aliasVar = null;
534 |       if (type == Token.NAME) {
535 |         String name = n.getString();
536 |         Var lexicalVar = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
537 |         if (lexicalVar != null && lexicalVar == aliases.get(name)) {
538 |           aliasVar = lexicalVar;
539 |         }
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       // Validate the top-level of the goog.scope block.
543 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() == 2) {
544 |         if (aliasVar != null && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
545 |           if (aliasVar.getNode() == n) {
546 |             aliasDefinitionsInOrder.add(n);
547 | 
548 |             // Return early, to ensure that we don't record a definition
549 |             // twice.
550 |             return;
551 |           } else {
552 |             report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_REDEFINED, n.getString());
553 |           }
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (type == Token.RETURN) {
557 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_RETURN);
558 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
559 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_REFERENCES_THIS);
560 |         } else if (type == Token.THROW) {
561 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_THROW);
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 | 
565 |       // Validate all descendent scopes of the goog.scope block.
566 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() >= 2) {
567 |         // Check if this name points to an alias.
568 |         if (aliasVar != null) {
569 |           // Note, to support the transitive case, it's important we don't
570 |           // clone aliasedNode here.  For example,
571 |           // var g = goog; var d = g.dom; d.createElement('DIV');
572 |           // The node in aliasedNode (which is "g") will be replaced in the
573 |           // changes pass above with "goog".  If we cloned here, we'd end up
574 |           // with <code>g.dom.createElement('DIV')</code>.
575 |           aliasUsages.add(new AliasedNode(aliasVar, n));
576 |         }
577 | 
578 |         // When we inject declarations, we duplicate jsdoc. Make sure
579 |         // we only process that jsdoc once.
580 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
581 |         if (info != null) {
582 |           for (Node node : info.getTypeNodes()) {
583 |             fixTypeNode(node);
584 |           }
585 |         }
586 | 
587 |         // TODO(robbyw): Error for goog.scope not at root.
588 |       }
589 |     }

980 |     @Override
981 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
982 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
983 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
984 |       }
985 | 
986 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
987 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
988 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
989 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
990 |             reportGetter(el);
991 |             continue;
992 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
993 |             reportSetter(el);
994 |             continue;
995 |           }
996 |         }
997 | 
998 |         AstNode rawKey = el.getLeft();
999 |         Node key = transformAsString(rawKey);
1000 |         key.setType(Token.STRING_KEY);
1001 |         if (rawKey instanceof Name && !isAllowedProp(key.getString())) {
1002 |           errorReporter.warning(INVALID_ES3_PROP_NAME, sourceName,
1003 |               key.getLineno(), "", key.getCharno());
1004 |         }
1005 | 
1006 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
1007 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
1008 |           key.setType(Token.GETTER_DEF);
1009 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
1010 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
1011 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
1012 |           }
1013 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
1014 |           key.setType(Token.SETTER_DEF);
1015 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
1016 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
1017 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
1018 |           }
1019 |         }
1020 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
1021 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
1022 |       }
1023 |       return node;
1024 |     }

===== M0700 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0715 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1197-1205
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1197 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1198 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1199 |       return -1;
1200 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1202 |     } else {
1203 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1204 |     }
1205 |   }

===== M0716 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.validateScopeCall(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Original range: 499-520
Documentation:

Method code:
499 |     private void validateScopeCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       if (preprocessorSymbolTable != null) {
501 |         preprocessorSymbolTable.addReference(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       }
503 |       if (!parent.isExprResult()) {
504 |         report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USED_IMPROPERLY);
505 |       }
506 |       if (n.getChildCount() != 2) {
507 |         // The goog.scope call should have exactly 1 parameter.  The first
508 |         // child is the "goog.scope" and the second should be the parameter.
509 |         report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_HAS_BAD_PARAMETERS);
510 |       } else {
511 |         Node anonymousFnNode = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
512 |         if (!anonymousFnNode.isFunction() ||
513 |             NodeUtil.getFunctionName(anonymousFnNode) != null ||
514 |             NodeUtil.getFunctionParameters(anonymousFnNode).hasChildren()) {
515 |           report(t, anonymousFnNode, GOOG_SCOPE_HAS_BAD_PARAMETERS);
516 |         } else {
517 |           scopeCalls.add(n);
518 |         }
519 |       }
520 |     }

===== M0729 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 57-193
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
57 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
58 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
59 |     int c;
60 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
61 |     for (;;) {
62 |       // eat white spaces
63 |       for (;;) {
64 |         charno = -1;
65 |         c = getChar();
66 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
67 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
68 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
70 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
71 |           break;
72 |         }
73 |       }
74 | 
75 |       switch (c) {
76 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
77 |         case '@':
78 |           do {
79 |             c = getChar();
80 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
81 |               addToString(c);
82 |             } else {
83 |               ungetChar(c);
84 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
85 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
86 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
87 |             }
88 |           } while (true);
89 | 
90 |         case '*':
91 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
92 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
93 |           } else {
94 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
95 |           }
96 | 
97 |         case ',':
98 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
99 | 
100 |         case '>':
101 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
102 | 
103 |         case '(':
104 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
105 | 
106 |         case ')':
107 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
108 | 
109 |         case '{':
110 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
111 | 
112 |         case '}':
113 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
114 | 
115 |         case '[':
116 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
117 | 
118 |         case ']':
119 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
120 | 
121 |         case '?':
122 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
123 | 
124 |         case '!':
125 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
126 | 
127 |         case ':':
128 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
129 | 
130 |         case '=':
131 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
132 | 
133 |         case '|':
134 |           matchChar('|');
135 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
136 | 
137 |         case '.':
138 |           c = getChar();
139 |           if (c == '<') {
140 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
141 |           } else {
142 |             if (c == '.') {
143 |               c = getChar();
144 |               if (c == '.') {
145 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
146 |               } else {
147 |                 addToString('.');
148 |               }
149 |             }
150 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
151 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
152 |             c = '.';
153 |           }
154 |           // fall through
155 | 
156 |         default: {
157 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
158 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
159 |           int c1 = c;
160 |           addToString(c);
161 |           int c2 = getChar();
162 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
163 |             ungetChar(c2);
164 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
165 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
166 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
167 |           } else {
168 |             do {
169 |               c1 = c2;
170 |               c2 = getChar();
171 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
172 |                 ungetChar(c2);
173 |                 ungetChar(c1);
174 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
175 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
176 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
177 |               } else {
178 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
179 |                   addToString(c1);
180 |                 } else {
181 |                   ungetChar(c2);
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
184 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
185 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
186 |                 }
187 |               }
188 |             } while (true);
189 |           }
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 |   }

===== M0731 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 241-286
Documentation:

Method code:
241 |   private boolean parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken token,
242 |                                   List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
243 |     while (true) {
244 |       switch (token) {
245 |         case ANNOTATION:
246 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
247 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
248 |             token = parseAnnotation(token, extendedTypes);
249 |           } else {
250 |             token = next();
251 |           }
252 |           break;
253 | 
254 |         case EOC:
255 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
256 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
257 |           }
258 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
259 |           return true;
260 | 
261 |         case EOF:
262 |           // discard any accumulated information
263 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
264 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
265 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
266 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
267 |           return false;
268 | 
269 |         case EOL:
270 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
271 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
272 |           }
273 |           token = next();
274 |           break;
275 | 
276 |         default:
277 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
278 |             token = next();
279 |           } else {
280 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
281 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
282 |           }
283 |           break;
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 |   }

/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.

2435 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {

791 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
792 |     boolean valid = false;
793 |     if (i == 1) {
794 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
795 |     } else if (i == 2) {
796 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
797 |     } else {
798 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
799 |     }
800 |

802 |       violation(
803 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
804 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
805 |     }

===== M0751 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

   * Detach a child from its parent and siblings.

Method code:
700 |   public void removeChild(Node child) {

702 |     if (prev == null) {
703 |       first = first.next;
